Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the superconductors object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 863. The moduli space has dimension at most 52 with 4 connected components.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the superconductors space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 863 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 52. QED.
